# Lifecycle requirement-to-evidence map

## Authority and completion rule

This map connects the [slice graph](README.md#slice-graph) to verification. The
[production plan](../../docs/production-hardening/production-hardening-tdd-plan-v2.md)
still owns requirements and thresholds. Grouped rows do not remove any detailed
acceptance case. A model property, a trace check and a native release gate are
different evidence; none can silently substitute for another.

The separate [2e/2f closure audit](closure-audit-2e-2f.md) owns their original
acceptance boundaries. This later-slice map does not reopen accepted accessors or
make every later requirement a consumer-closure prerequisite; the audit identifies
the protected-custody/native-exclusion subsets only where an original schedule
actually depends on retirement.

Each acceptance record must bind the requirement IDs, exact implementation and
model bytes, model command/seed/domain, observed implementation trace, checker
bytes, selected native tests, terminal results and independent disposition.
Record missing evidence as OPEN, an unreached prerequisite as UNREACHED, and a
counterexample as RED. Do not aggregate unrelated successful runs into one pass.

Preserve already accepted evidence and run only affected requirements. A source
change needs a documented dependency analysis before old evidence carries over;
equal output hashes establish identity, not broader semantic coverage. New
acceptance runs follow model/trace review where practical. Causal diagnostic RED
and native characterization may precede the model when needed to discover the
real transition boundary; they are not acceptance runs.

## Current gameplay diagnosis scope

The receipt's `finiteMaintenanceEvidenceReuse20261018` owns the bounded
maintenance-lifetime implementation and its affected safety, exact-accounting,
matched native and unprofiled evidence. Immutable syntax and signed facts may
outlive one stage; native currency, progress and authority may not. This removes
measured duplication but does not close multi-second rollover latency, growing
later-epoch costs, supported64 or endurance requirements. The same receipt entry
classifies the finite remaining implementation and execution gaps.

The receipt's `causalRolloverDiagnosis20261018` owns the current API-local counter,
allocation/GC and semantic-stage attribution. First-entry command labels are not
causal intervals: exact API timestamps supersede the earlier readiness-heavy
adoption explanation. Native scheduler attempts failed finalization/coverage and
are not evidence of storage waits or contention. The finite implementation
handoff remains separate from accepted evidence and original acceptance gates.

multi-second sealing/adoption, later-epoch growth and the separate supported64

## Existing models: actual scope

- `packages/protocol-v3/formal/registry-signed-envelope-variables.qnt` names
  registered fields. It contains no lifecycle transition system or liveness
  proof. Registry correspondence is not authentication or recovery acceptance.
- `packages/protocol-v3/formal/author-lineage-actions.qnt` supplies existing
  `authorSequenceAuthenticated`, `successfulLocalIssuanceContiguous`,
  `failedLocalIssuanceOpaque`, `localIssuedRecordsBindSelectedSequence`,
  `equivocationObservationSound` and `boundedStateFinite` properties. Its finite
  epoch-zero action domain does not establish retirement fences across epochs.
- `packages/seal/formal/seal-safety.qnt` and `seal-pacemaker.qnt` supply existing
  `agreement`, `lockSafety`, `authorityIsolation`, `durableBeforeGossip`,
  `finalityRequiresCommitQc`, `highestQcRestartSafe` and `boundedFutureEvidence`
  under their respective domains. The pacemaker's `reachedFairFinality` is a
  witness under its `fairStep` schedule, not universal eventual finality during
  indefinite loss or a proof of signing-store compaction.
- The frozen [bounded lifecycle model](</private/tmp/ts-drp-lifecycle-integration-high-u5HdMQ/.logs/bounded-storage-lifecycle/combined-integration-01/e18-f18g-native-copy-cleanup-input-02/manifest.json>)
  is linked by the controlled integration manifest, which pins its exact external
  artifact paths and hashes. Its `safety`, `nativeCertified` and `heldCurrent`
  separate operation-private readback from later held-source lifetime. Its
  `readbackWitness`, `stagedWithHeldSources` and `twoRotations` are finite
  witnesses. Authentication, bytes, native transactions, crash intermediates,
  incarnation churn, concurrency and scheduler fairness are abstracted. The
  earlier fifteen cases and sampled thousand forty-step traces remain accepted
  only in that narrow scope; they are not full coverage or implementation trace
  conformance.
- The existing Phase-3 exit model/driver is a useful implementation-observation
  seam for admission, journal/outbox and restart effects. It is a TypeScript
  model, not a Quint lifecycle proof. Preserve its accepted scenarios rather
  than duplicating their authority in a new product adapter.

The new property names below are **required extension targets**, not assertions
that those symbols or proofs already exist. Only a source-bound model result
and disposition can change that status.

## Trace conformance contract

Use existing owned observers or additive test instrumentation that cannot mint
authority or alter product transitions. Any added test-only read/await must be
explicitly identified as a fixture observation-schedule change. Capture actual pre/post durable observations and
event outcomes; distinguish invocation, native completion and promise delivery.
Normalize identities and byte charges only with a documented, checked mapping.
Preserve refusal codes and uncertain outcomes. A missing observation is not an
implicit successful model step. Check every intermediate state and the complete
action sequence, not just the final digest or one selected witness.

Pin the trace, model, normalizer/checker, implementation and relevant runtime
dependencies. Causal mutations must detect omitted/reordered/extra actions,
wrong identity/revision/charge and false success. A test that merely replays a
handwritten Quint trace establishes model execution, not implementation
conformance. Native process-death traces may end at a crash and resume in another
process; joining promises or injecting throws is a different evidence class.

For progress, record environmental assumptions separately from safety: fair
maintenance, eventual store availability, sufficient honest communication and
supported authority. Existential reachability, bounded fair simulation and
temporal/exhaustive proof have different status. Never call an enabled action or
successful sample a universal liveness theorem.

### R8 workload-capacity resource measurements

A bounded game may remain in one epoch for its entire session. Variable-sized
actions and same-Writer batches need only stay within enforced operation,
resulting-state, dependency and outstanding-work bounds; rollover is not a
per-action requirement. Capacity is established at explicit preparation
boundaries, and supported ordinary calls meter new obligations without building
hypothetical future scenarios. Oversized or over-capacity new requests refuse
before signing or durable acceptance. Already accepted work remains protected,
recoverable and finishable. Separate multi-epoch recovery requirements must not
put whole-context forecasting back onto this single-epoch critical path.

Prepared finishing capacity is a local admission guarantee, not a promise to
admit every workload permitted by authenticated protocol maxima. The existing
application resource owner must bound actual operation/batch effects and state,
while the shared owner includes protected sources and temporary overlap through
sealing, adoption, settlement/recovery and lawful cleanup. Each new proof still
pays its complete cumulative KNOWN and UNKNOWN charges. Numerical preparedness
is neither source authentication nor a portable allowance: its absence must not
invalidate a source or trigger repeated source priming. Progress assumes the
required dependencies, signing/storage service and lawful recovery remain
available; it does not promise indefinite new admission or bound physical memory.
The prepared whole-window certificate is a sufficient shortcut, not a narrower
replacement for the enforced declaration. When that shortcut is inconclusive,
the existing prepared numerical program may check the actual candidate or whole
local suffix under the same hard limits. A numerical miss refuses before effects;
it must not reconstruct future scenarios or re-prime source authority. This path
remains opt-in local application configuration, not the default for every game.
Lawfully retired classified history is not a missing live application base: only
the original certificate's positive retirement fact can discharge that historical
alternative. Current, rollback, operational, installed and physically present
bases remain complete; fresh native currency and full proof charges still apply.

### R8 forecast contract and byte accounting

The genuine pending path has a confirmed implementation gap, not merely missing
execution: native103 publishes the successor while inherited classification is
still held. The [existing MAIN receipt](../../.logs/bounded-storage-lifecycle/phase6-continuation-01/r8-native-main-integration01/receipt.json)
preserves the original failure and pins three new finite publication controls.
They cover held-debt refusal, fresh SAME-intent continuation and stale-source
refusal, including rejected broken transitions. Native authentication,
reservation/join, storage atomicity and bytes remain premises; these checks do
not close the repaired native-publication requirement. The next execution must
observe readiness before actual publication and prove real cold continuation.

The forecast is a **pre-sign admission condition**, not retirement authority or
a universal progress theorem. The original live issuer awaits the creator's
maintenance quote and held-source currency checks before entering its native
unsigned-intent reservation, durable ENTERED marker and signer. Its intended
obligation is that this exact admitted operation can finish its native job and
leave the supported complete close/adoption, protected recovery and capacity
maintenance continuations representable under the original limits. Storage
availability, authenticated eligibility, currentness and serviced maintenance
remain premises; the completed original public continuations do not discharge
the separate complete resource and authenticated-migration requirements.

The fixed observer entitlement is **cumulative operation-local encoded proof
charges**: distinct complete known byte sequences deduplicate by exact equality;
every unknown occurrence contributes separately. Release does not refund that
ledger. A subsequent joined acquisition starts a fresh ledger; complete legal
alternative lifetimes must each fit, rather than summing every serial job into
one lifetime. Native image/row/cardinality checks are separate uses of original
bounds. Neither charge arithmetic nor a native image ceiling bounds total scan
work, peak JavaScript heap, native cloning, RSS or physical database allocation.
The [accounting and forecast owner](../../packages/node/src/internal/creator-closed-rollback-data.ts)
owns these distinctions.

current native job. They do not reserve the byte entitlement of future proofs.
The existing detached fold/export/close preparation already supplies exact
current source-owned encodings without mutating live state. Future signatures,
QC/control digests and reacquired classifications do not all exist yet, so their
complete encodings retain conservative widths and no assumed identity credit.
Executing future installs as a preflight could advance durable floors/controls
or delete detail, and cannot replace this read-only condition. A tighter bound
must come from demonstrated source-owned encoding/correlation, not removing the
forecast or treating current custody as future capacity.
